(क) एक संविदाकारी सरकार, के अनुरोध पर महानिदेशक, किसी जलयान का सर्वेक्षण कराएगा और यदि वह संतुष्ट हो 
कि संशोधित अभिसमय के उपबंधों का अनुपालन किया गया है, तो वह जलयान को अंतर्राष्ट्रीय लोड लाइन HPAI 966, 
जैसा कि इसके 988 प्रोटोकॉल द्वारा संशोधित किया गया है, के अनुसार अंतर्राष्ट्रीय लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र जारी करेगा 
या जारी करने के लिए प्राधिकृत करेगा; 

(ख) उस प्रमाणपत्र की एक कॉपी, शीर्षान्तर की गिनती के लिए प्रयोग की गई सर्वे रिपोर्ट की एक कॉपी, और गिनती 
की एक कॉपी जितनी जल्दी हो सके महानिदेशक को भेजी जाएगी; और 

(ग) इस प्रकार जारी किए गए प्रमाणपत्र में इस आशय का कथन होगा कि यह भारत सरकार के अनुरोध पर जारी 
किया गया है, जिसका झंडा जलयान फहरा रहा है या फहराएगा और, इसका वही बल होगा और इसे इन नियमों के अधीन 
जारी प्रमाणपत्र के समान ही मान्यता प्राप्त होगी | 

(3) एकाधिक लोड लाइनें निम्नलिखित परिस्थितियों औरतरीके से आबंटित की जा सकती हैं, अर्थात्‌ :- 

(क) जहां किसी जलयान को अलग-अलग सेवा कंडीशन, सीज़नल ज़ोन या ट्रेडिंग पैटर्न के अधीन ऑपरेट करना है, वहां

9. प्रमाणपत्र की अवधि.- इन नियमों के अधीन जारी किया गया प्रत्येक लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र, समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी 
द्वारा निर्धारित दिनांक तक विधिमान्य होगा, जो नियम 5 के अधीन जलयान के सर्वेक्षण के पूरा होने की दिनांक के 
पश्चात्‌ की दिनांक से पांच वर्ष से अधिक की दिनांक नहीं होगी और ऐसी दिनांक समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी द्वारा उसके द्वारा 
जारी किए गए प्रत्येक प्रमाणपत्र में विनिर्दिष्ट की जाएगी, ऐसा न होने पर प्रमाणपत्र विधिमान्य प्रमाणपत्र नहीं माना 
जाएगा | 

0. लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र का विस्तार.-- (i) जहां किसी जलयान, जिसके सम्बन्ध में लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र प्रभावी है, 
का सर्वेक्षण स्वामी द्वारा वर्तमान प्रमाणपत्र की समाप्ति पर प्रभावी होने वाले नए प्रमाणपत्र जारी करने के लिए किए गए 
आवेदन के पश्चात किया गया है, वहां समनुदेशक प्राधिकारी या सर्वेक्षक,- 

(क) अगर सर्वेक्षण की जांच से यह पक्का हो जाए कि जलयान इन नियमों के भाग 4और समनुदेशन की शर्तों से जुड़े 
पहली अनुसूची की लागू अपेक्षाओं को पूरा करता है; या 

(ख) महानिदेशक से यह सूचना प्राप्त होने पर कि जलयान स्थिरता से संबंधित तीसरी अनुसूची की आवश्यकताओं का 
अनुपालन करता है; 

(ग) यदि वह समझता है कि नया प्रमाणपत्र जारी करना यथोचित रूप से व्यावहारिक नहीं है, तो जलयान के वर्तमान 
प्रमाणपत्रों की विधिमान्यता को पांच महीने से अधिक अवधि के लिए बढ़ा सकता है और ऐसे प्रत्येक मामले में, वर्तमान 
प्रमाणपत्र की विधिमान्यता की बढ़ी हुई दिनांक की समाप्ति के पश्चात्‌ एक नया प्रमाणपत्र जारी किया जाएगा | 

(2) उपनियम (॥) के अधीन प्रदान किया गया ऐसा कोई विस्तार तब तक प्रभावी नहीं होगा, जब तक कि प्रमाणपत्र 
की विधिमान्यता की अवधि बढ़ाने की दिनांक का विवरण, साथ ही विस्तार प्रदान किए जाने वाले स्थान और दिनांक का 
विवरण, वर्तमान प्रमाणपत्र पर समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी या सर्वेक्षक द्वारा पृष्ठांकित नहीं कर दिया जाता | 

(3) उपनियम (i) के अधीन किसी जलयान को अंततः जारी किये गये नये प्रमाणपत्र की विधिमान्यता अवधि sea 
उपनियम में निर्दिष्ट सर्वेक्षण पूरा होने की दिनांक से पांच वर्ष से अधिक नहीं होगी | 

4. लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्रों का रद्दीकरण.-- () जहां महानिदेशक, समनुदेशन प्राधिकरण या सर्वेक्षक की रिपोर्ट या 
अन्यथा से संतुष्ट हो कि - 

(क) जिस जलयान से प्रमाणपत्र जुड़ा है, वह समनुदेशन की शर्तों को पूरा नहीं करता है; या 

(ख) जलयान की बनावट की ताकत इतनी कम हो जाए कि जलयान असुरक्षित हो जाए; या 

(ग) जिस जानकारी के आधार पर जल्यान को शीर्षान्तर दिए गए थे, वह आवश्यक मामलों में Ted साबित हुई है; या 

(घ)नियम (2 के उपनियम () के खंड (क) में उल्लिखित फिटिंग्स तथा उपकरणों को प्रभावी स्थिति में नहीं रखा जाता 
है, तो महानिदेशक इन नियमों के अधीन जारी किसी लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र को रद्द कर सकता है अथवा रद्द करवा सकता 
है | 
2) जहां महानिदेशक के संज्ञान में यह बात आती है कि- 

क) किसी जलयान का प्रमाणपत्र नियम (2 के अनुसार पृष्ठांकित नहीं किया गया है ; या 

ख) जलयान के संबंध में नया प्रमाणपत्र जारी किया जाता है; या

2. पोतों का समय-समय पर निरीक्षण.- () प्रत्येक जलयान जिसके संबंध A लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र लागू है, का इस 
नियम के उपबंधों के अनुसार एक सर्वेक्षक दवारा समय-समय पर निरीक्षण किया जाएगा जिससे यह सुनिश्चित किया जा 
सके कि- 

(क) जलयान मेँ खुली जगहों, गार्ड रेल, HUSA पत्तन और क्रू के क्वार्टर तक पहुँचने के साधनों की सुरक्षा के लिए फिटिंग 
और उपस्कर अच्छी हालत में हैं; और 

(ख) जलयान के हल या सुपरस्ट्रक्चर में कोई परिवर्तन नहीं किया गया है जिससे कोई गलत डेटा निकल सकता है 
जिसके आधार पर जलयान को शीर्षान्तर दिए गए हैं | 

(2) समय-समय पर होने वाले निरीक्षण के लिए हर आवेदन जलयान के स्वामी की ओर से समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी को 
किया जाएगा, जो निरीक्षण करने के लिए एक सर्वेयर नियुक्त करेगा | 

(3) सर्वेक्षक ऐसे किसी निरीक्षण के दौरान ऐसे निरीक्षण को कराने की आवश्यकता बता सकता है जो उसकी राय में 
यह स्थापित करने के लिए आवश्यक हों कि जलयान उपनियम (I) की अपेक्षाओं का अनुपालन करता है। 

(4) इस नियम के अधीन अपेक्षित समय-समय पर होने वाले निरीक्षण, जलयान के सर्वे के पूरा होने की दिनांक की हर 
आब्दिकी पर, जिससे उसका विद्यमान लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र जारी होता है, हर वर्ष नौ से पंद्रह महीने के समय में किए 
जाएंगे | यह निरीक्षण उस दिनांक से तीन महीने पहले शुरू होगा और तीन महीने के पश्चात्‌ समाप्त होगा | 

परंतु महानिदेशक, यदि संतुष्ट हो कि परिस्थितियों की आवश्यकता है, तो वह वार्षिक निरीक्षण को विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि 
से पहले या उसके पश्चात्‌ किए जाने की अनुमति दे सकता है | 

(5) यदि सर्वेक्षक निरीक्षण के पश्चात्‌ संतुष्ट हो जाता है कि जलयान उपनियम (4) की अपेक्षाओं का अनुपालन करता 
है तो वह लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र में इस प्रयोजन के लिए दिए गए स्थान में ऐसे निरीक्षण का अभिलेख पृष्ठांकित करेगा 
और प्रमाणित करेगा कि- 

(क) अंतरराष्ट्रीय लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र (966) के मामले में, यह पाया गया कि जलयान अभिसमय के संबंधित नियमों 
का पालन करता है; और 

(ख) इंडिया लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र के मामले में, यह पाया गया कि जलयान इन नियमों के संबंधित उपबंधों का पालन 
करता है | 

(6) ऐसे प्रत्येक पृष्ठांकन पर निरीक्षण करने वाले सर्वेयर की दिनांक और हस्ताक्षर होंगे, जिसमें वह समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी 
विनिर्दिष्ट होगा जिसकी ओर से निरीक्षण किया गया था | 


3. छूट. - () पड़ोसी पत्तनों को छूट: महानिदेशक दो या दो से अधिक राज्यों के निकटवर्ती पड़ोसी पत्तनों के बीच 
पूरी तरह से अंतरराष्ट्रीय यात्राओं में लगे किसी जलयान को इन नियमों और अभिसमय के उपबंधों से छूट दे सकता है, 
यह तब जब कि- 

(क) जिन राज्यों में ऐसे पत्तन हैं, उनकी सरकारें इस बात से सहमत हैं कि ऐसी यात्राओं की सुरक्षित प्रकृति या दशा 
अभिसमय के नियमों को लागूकरना गलत या व्यवहारिक नहीं बनाती हैं; और 

(ख) जलयान केवल ऐसी यात्राओं पर ही लगा रहता है | 

(2) नए जलयान या प्रयोगात्मक फ़ीचर्स से छूट: महानिदेशक किसी ऐसे जलयान को, जिसमें नए तरह के फ़ीचर्स हों, 
इन नियमों या अभिसमय की किसी भी अपेक्षा से छूट दे सकता है, जहां ऐसी अपेक्षा के लागू होने से ऐसे फ़ीचर्स के 
विकास में शोध में गंभीर रुकावट आएगी: 

परंतु- 

(क) महानिदेशक इस बात से संतुष्ट हैं कि जलयान उस सेवा के लिए आवश्यक सुरक्षा अपेक्षाओं को पूरा करता है 
जिसके लिए इसे बनाया गया है; और 

(ख) ऐसी अपेक्षाएं अभिसमय के अधीन दिए गए स्तर से कम सुरक्षा का समग्र स्तर सुनिश्चित करती हैं; और

(ग) यह छूट उन राज्यों की सरकारों को प्रतिग्राहय है जहां जलयान जाना चाहता है | 

(3) एकलत्र अंतर्राष्ट्रीय यात्रा छूट: एक जलयान जो सामान्यतः अंतर्राष्ट्रीय यात्राओं पर नहीं जाता है, उसे असाधारण 
परिस्थितियों में, महानिदेशक द्वारा एकल अंतर्राष्ट्रीय यात्रा करने के उद्देश्य से इन नियमों या अभिसमयकी किसी भी 
अपेक्षा से छूट दी जा सकती है, यह तब जब जलयान उस विशिष्ट यात्रा के लिए पर्याप्त सुरक्षा अपेक्षाओं का अनुपालन 
करता हो | 

(4) अंतरराष्ट्रीय समुद्री संगठन को अधिसूचना: महानिदेशक, उपनियम (), उपनियम (2) या उपनियम(3) के अधीन 
दी गई प्रत्येक छूट का विवरण, साथ ही ऐसी छूट देने के कारण, अंतरराष्ट्रीय समुद्री संगठन को संविदा करने वाली सरकारों 
को प्रसारित करने के लिए सूचित करेगा। 

(5) छूट की शर्तें: इस नियम के अधीन दी गई कोई भी छूट-

4. Be प्रमाणपत्र . - (|) जहां महानिदेशक अधिनियम के उपबंधों के अनुसार किसी भी जलयान को छूट देता है, 
महानिदेशक ऐसे जलयान के संबंध में पांचवीं अनुसूची में निर्धारित प्ररूप में अंतर्राष्ट्रीय लोड लाइन छूट प्रमाणपत्र जारी 
करेगा |

(ख) अगर सर्वेयर निरीक्षण के पश्चात्‌ इस बात से संतुष्ट हो जाता है कि जलयान उन शर्तों का पालन कर रहा है

को लगाने, या किसी अन्य उपबंध को जलयान पर अंगीकृत की अनुमति दे सकते हैं, यदि परीक्षण द्वारा या अन्यथा 
संतुष्ट हो कि ऐसा फिटिंग, सामग्री, उपस्कर, तंत्र या उपबंध कम से कम विनिर्दिष्ट अपेक्षा के समान प्रभावी है |

वह सुरक्षा का समतुल्य स्तर सुनिश्चित करने के लिए आवश्यक समझें |

6. प्रायोगिक उद्देश्य के लिए मंज़्री.- (4) महानिदेशक किसी भी जल्यान के सम्बन्ध में, जिस पर अभिसमय लागू 
होता है, प्रायोगिक उददेश्यों के लिए विशिष्ट अनुमोदन प्रदान कर सकता है, जहां ऐसा अनुमोदन नवीन डिजाइनों, 
व्यवस्थाओं, या सुरक्षा सुविधाओं के विकास को सुविधाजनक बनाने के लिए आवश्यक माना जाता है | 

(2) उपनियम (4) के अधीन प्रदान किया गया कोई भी अनुमोदन महानिदेशक द्वारा निर्दिष्ट ऐसी शर्तों के अधीन होगा, 
जिससे यह सुनिश्चित किया जा सके कि जलयान की सुरक्षा का समग्र स्तर कम न हो | 

(3) महानिदेशक, इस नियम के अधीन दी गई हर मंज़ूरी की जानकारी संविदा करने वाली सरकारों को बताने के लिए 
अंतरराष्ट्रीयसमुद्री संगठन को बताएंगे |

के अधीन उस जलयान पर लागू थीं और एक विद्यमान जलयान, एक नियम के तौर पर, नए जलयान के लिए अपेक्षाओं 
का उससे कम पालन नहीं करेगा जितना उसने ऐसी मरम्मत, परिवर्तन या संशोधन से पहले किया था |

अधीन दिए गए निर्देशों में यह निर्देश सम्मिलित कर सकता है कि डेक-लाइन को जलयान के किनारे पर किसी अन्य बिंदु 
के संदर्भ में चिहिनत किया जा सकता है जो उपनियम (2) में निर्दिष्ट स्थिति के यथासंभव निकटतम है और संदर्भ बिंदु 
का स्थान और शीर्षान्तर डेक की पहचान सभी मामल्रों में अंतर्राष्ट्रीय लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र पर इंगित की जाएगी |

25. लोड लाइनें.-- () उपनियम (2) और नियम 26 में विनिर्दिष्ट लोड लाइनें अधिकतम गहराई दर्शाती समझी जाएंगी,

उनके साथ चिहिनत किसी जलयान पर लोड किया जा सकता है |

मिलीमीटर लंबी और 25 मिलीमीटर चौड़ी क्षैतिज रेखाओं से मिलकर बनेंगी जो लोड लाइन चिह्न के वलय के केंद्र से 540 
मिलीमीटर आगे और उस रेखा से समकोण पर चिहिनित 25 मिलीमीटर चौड़ी ऊर्ध्वाधर रेखा के आगे या पीछे तक फैली 
होंगी |

ऊर्ध्वाधर नीचे की ओर मापी गई दूरी जल्यान को सौंपे गए शीर्षान्तर के बराबर हो, जो उस लोड लाइन के लिए उपयुक्त 
हो |

30. उचित चिहनों को हटाने आदि का प्राधिकार.. किसी जलयान पर उचित चिहन लगा दिए जाने के पश्चात्‌, ऐसे 
चिहनों को छिपाया, बिगाड़ा या मिटाया नहीं जाएगा और उन्हें समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी की पूर्व स्वीकृति के बिना हटाया या 
बदला नहीं जाएगा | 

3i. समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी का चिहन.- () उपनियम (2) में विनिर्दिष्ट समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी का चिहन जलयान के 
प्रत्येक ओर लोड रेखा चिह्न के साथ-साथ, उस चिहन का भाग बनाने वाली क्षैतिज रेखा के ऊपर या उसके ऊपर और नीचे 
अंकित किया जाएगा | 

(2) समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी के चिहन में उनके नाम की पहचान के लिए चार से ज़्यादा आध्याक्षर नहीं होंगे, हर एक की 
ऊंचाई लगभग 45 मिमी और चौड़ाई 75 मिमी होगी |

अन्यथा अपेक्षित हो |

33. समनुदेशन की शर्तों का अनुपालन.- (!) उपनियम (2) में अन्यथा उपबंधित के सिवाय, किसी जलयान को

(ख) नियम 34 के उपनियम (3) के अनुसार जलयान पर समनुदेशन की शर्तों का अभिलेख नहीं रखा जाता है |

बात से संतुष्ट है कि परिवर्तन ऐसा नहीं है कि जलयान को सौंपे गए शीर्षान्तर में किसी भी तरह के परिवर्तन की 
आवश्यकता हो और परिवर्तन का पूरा ब्यौरा, निरीक्षण की दिनांक और जगह के साथ, सर्वेयर ने समनुदेशन की शर्तों के 
अभिलेख पर पृष्ठांकित कर दिया है |

34. समनुदेशन की शर्तों का अभिलेख.- (I) किसी जलयान के, जिसे शीर्षान्तर सौंपे गए हैं, हल, अधिरचना, फिटिंग 
और उपकरणों के संबंध में सौंपे जाने की शर्तों का अभिलेख चौथी अनुसूची में दिए गए प्ररूप में या परिस्थितियों के अनुसार 
उसके निकटतम प्ररूप में होगा और उसमें उस प्ररूप द्वारा अपेक्षित विवरण सम्मिलित होंगे और ऐसे विवरण सर्वेक्षण 
रिपोर्ट की एक प्रति अभिलेख के साथ संलग्न करके और उस रिपोर्ट के उन अंशों को अभिलेख में निर्दिष्ट करके दिए जा 
सकते हैं जिनमें सुसंगत विवरण दिए गए हैं | 

(2) नियम 5 के अनुसार जलयान का सर्वेक्षण करने वाले सर्वेक्षक द्वारा अभिलेख पूरा किया जाएगा और नियम 6 के 
उपबंधों के अनुसार उसे समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी को उपलब्ध कराया जाएगा और समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी द्वारा अभिलेख की दो 
प्रतियां जलयान के स्वामी को नियम 7 द्वारा इस प्रकार उपलब्ध कराए जाने के लिए अपेक्षित विवरण और निर्देशों के 
साथ भेजी जाएंगी | 

(3) समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी दवारा जलयान के स्वामी को दिए गए समनुदेशन की शर्तें, ब्यौरे और निर्देशों के अभिलेख 
की एक प्रति, प्रत्येक समय जलयान के स्वामी की अभिरक्षा में जलयान पर रखी जाएगी |

35. शीर्षान्तर के प्रकार. नियमों के अधीन किसी भी जलयान को सौँपे जाने वाले शीर्षान्तर निम्नलिखित होंगे- 
) ग्रीष्मकालीन शीर्षान्तर; 
2) उष्णकटिबंधीय शीर्षान्तर; 
3) शीतकालीन शीर्षान्तर; 
4) शीतकालीन उत्तर अटलांटिक शीर्षान्तर; 
5) ताजे पानी का शीर्षान्तर; 
6) उष्णकटिबंधीय ताजा पानी शीर्षान्तर; 
7) ग्रीष्मकालीन काष्ठ शीर्षान्तर; 
8) शीतकालीन काष्ठशीर्षान्तर; 
9) शीतकालीन उत्तर अटलांटिक काष्ठ शीर्षान्तर; 
0) उष्णकटिबंधीय काष्ठ शीर्षान्तर;

36. शीर्षान्तर का निर्धारण.- नियम 37 में अन्यथा उपबंधित के सिवाय,- 

(क) नए जलयान को दिए जाने वाले शीर्षान्तर का फैसला दूसरी अनुसूची के नियमों के अनुसार किया जाएगा; और 

(ख) किसी विद्यमान जलयान Ht A जाने वाले शीर्षान्तर का निर्धारण, इन नियमों के लागू होने से तुरन्त पहले 
लागू नियमों के अधीन जल्रयान पर उस संबंध में लागू उपबन्धों के अनुसार किया जाएगा: 

परंतु यदि किसी विद्यमान जलयान को इस तरह बनाया गया है या उसमें परिवर्तन किया गया है जिससे वह अपनी 
तरह के नए जलयान पर लागू होने वाली पहली अनुसूची की अपेक्षाओं को पूरा कर सके, और ऐसे जलयान के संबंध में 
दूसरी अनुसूची के उपबंधों के अनुसार तय किए गए शीर्षान्तर के समनुदेशन के लिए आवेदन किया जाता है, तो ऐसे 
Asia जलयान को समनुदेशित किए जा सकते हैं |

38. डेक-लाइन की विशेष स्थिति और शीर्षातर का सुधार.-- किसी भी मामले में, जहां नियम 23 के उपनियम (3) के 
अनुसार जलयान के किनारों पर डेक-लाइन को चिह्न किया जाना है, जलयान को दिए जाने वाले शीर्षान्तर को उस 
उपनियम के कारण डेक-लाइन की स्थिति में होने वाली सीधी दूरी को ध्यान में रखते हुए ठीक किया जाएगा और उस 
पॉइंट की जगह जिसके हिसाब से डेक-लाइन को इस तरह चिह्न किया गया है और उस SH की पहचान जिसे शीर्षीन्तर 
डेक माना गया है, ऐसे जलयान के लिए जारी लोड लाइन प्रमाणपत्र में बताई जाएगी।

स्थिरता और लोडिंग 

39. पोतों की स्थिरता के बारे में जानकारी.- () प्रत्येक जलयान का स्वामी, जिसे इन नियमों के अधीन शीर्षान्तर 
आबंटित किए गए हैं, जलयान के स्वामी के मार्गदर्शन के लिए, इस नियम के उपबन्धों के अनुसार जलयान की स्थिरता 
से संबंधित जानकारी उपलब्ध कराएगा |

लिए उपयुक्त स्थिरता विवरण सम्मिलित होंगे और गणना की विधि और विवरण का स्वरूप जहां तक संभव हो, उस 
अनुसूची के भाग 3 अथवा उसके समतुल्य के अनुसार होगा |

संहिता, 2008 (आईएस संहिता 2008) में बताए गए मानदंड के अनुसार होंगी | 

(4) उपनियम (5) के उपबंधों के अधीन, जब सूचना पहली बार प्रदान की जाती है, तो वह एक झुकाव परीक्षण के 
माध्यम से स्थिरता के निर्धारण पर आधारित होगी, जो कि जब तक समनुदेशन प्राधिकारी अन्यथा अनुमति न दे, 
महानिदेशक द्वारा नियुक्त सर्वेक्षक की उपस्थिति में किया जाएगा और पहली बार प्रदान की गई सूचना को नई सूचना से 
बदल दिया जाएगा, जब भी जलयान के परिवर्तन से इसकी सटीकता पर भौतिक रूप से प्रभाव पड़ता है, जो कि महानिदेशक 
द्वारा अपेक्षित होने पर एक और झुकाव परीक्षण के आधार पर होगा |

(क) किसी भी जलयान के मामले में, जानकारी को इनक्लाइनिंग परीक्षण के द्वारा सिस्टर जलयान की स्थिरता के 
पता लगाने पर आधारित होने दें; या

गया हो, झुकाव परीक्षण की ज़रूरत नहीं होगी, यदि वैसे ही पोतों के बारे में विद्यमान जानकारी से महानिदेशक को यकीन

प्रति के साथ प्रस्तुत की जाएगी और उसमें ऐसे परिवर्धन और संशोधन सम्मित्रित किए जाएंगे, जैसा कि महानिदेशक 
किसी विशेष मामले में अपेक्षित करें | 

(7) इस नियम के अधीन दी गई जानकारी जलयान के स्वामी को एक किताब के रूप में स्वामी को देनी होगी, जिसे 
जलयान के स्वामी की अभिरक्षा में हर समय जलयान पर रखा जाएगा | 

(8) अगर किसी जलयान में कोई ऐसा परिवर्तन किया जाता है जिससे स्वामी को दी गई लोडिंग या स्थिरता की 
जानकारी पर कोई विशेष असर पड़ता है, तो बदली हुई जानकारी दी जाएगी, और अगर आवश्यक हो, तो जलयान को फिर 
से झुकाया जाएगा |

चेतावनी डिवाइस लगा होना चाहिए ताकि मशीनरी के सामान्य प्रचालन से निकले पानी के अतिरिक्त, मशीनरी स्पेस में 
पानी के किसी भी प्रवेश के बारे में सही नियंत्रण स्थिति पर चेतावनी दी जा सके | 


(6) इस उप-पैरा में “अनअटेंडेड मशीनरी स्पेस” से ऐसी मशीनरी स्पेस अभप्रेत है जो समुद्र में 
जलयान के सामान्य प्रचालन के दौरान किसी भी समय के लिए बिना लोगों के हो और “As मशीनरी 
स्पेस” से ऐसी मशीनरी स्पेस अभप्रेत है जो बिना लोगों के अनअटेंडेंट स्पेस के अतरिक्ति हो।

देता हो, जिसकी सतह फिसलन-रहित हो, और जिसके दोनों तरफ पूरी लंबाई में गार्ड रेल लगी हों | गार्ड रेल कम से कम

ब्रेकवाटर लगाए जाएँगे |

(2) Sh पर रखे जाने वाले लकड़ी के माल को कम से कम पूरी उपलब्ध लंबाई तक फैलाया जाना चाहिए; यह लंबाई 
सुपरस्ट्रक्चर (जहाज़ के ऊपरी ढांचे) के बीच बने वेल (खुले हिस्से) या वेलों की कुल लंबाई के बराबर होती है | जहाँ पिछले 
सिरे पर कोई सीमा तय करने वाला सुपरस्ट्रक्चर नहीं होता, वहाँ लकड़ी को कम से कम सबसे पिछले Sad (माल चढ़ाने- 
उतारने के रास्ते) के पिछले सिरे तक फैलाया जाएगा | 


डेक पर रखे जाने वाले लकड़ी के माल को जहाज़ के किनारे तक जितना हो सके, उतना चौड़ाई में फैलाया जाएगा ; इसमें 
गार्ड रेल, बुल्वार्क Fea, सीधे खंभे, पायलट के आने-जाने के रास्ते जैसी रुकावटों के लिए उचित जगह छोड़ी जाएगी | 
परंतु इस तरह जहाज़ के किनारे पर बनने वाला कोई भी खाली स्थान, जहाज़ की चौड़ाई के औसत 4% से ज़्यादा नहीं 
होगा | लकड़ी को जितना हो सके, उतना ठोस तरीके से जमाकर रखा जाएगा, और इसकी ऊँचाई कम से कम सुपरस्ट्रक्चर 
की मानक ऊँचाई के बराबर होगी | 


(3) सर्दियों में, मौसमी शीत क्षेत्र के भीतर किसी जहाज़ पर, मौसम के संपर्क में आने वाले SH के ऊपर SH कार्गो की 
ऊँचाई, जहाज़ की अधिकतम चौड़ाई के एक-तिहाई से अधिक नहीं होगी | 


(4) लकड़ी के डेक कार्गो को सघन रूप से जमाया (stow) जाएगा, बांधा (lash) जाएगा और सुरक्षित किया जाएगा | 
यह किसी भी तरह से जहाज़ के नेविगेशन और ज़रूरी कामों में बाधा नहीं डालेगा | 


(5) लकड़ी की प्रकृति के अनुसार जब भी ज़रूरत हो, अपराइट्स (सीधे Ga) जहाज़ की चौड़ाई को ध्यान में रखते हुए 
पर्याप्त मज़बूती वाले होंगे ; अपराइट्स की मज़बूती बुल्वार्क (जहाज़ की रेलिंग) की मज़बूती से ज़्यादा नहीं होंगे और उनके 
बीच की दूरी ले जाई जा रही लकड़ी की लंबाई और प्रकार के हिसाब से सही होंगी, लेकिन 3 मीटर से ज़्यादा नहीं होंगी | 
अपराइट्स को सुरक्षित करने के लिए मज़बूत एंगल्स, धातु के सॉकेट्स, या उतने ही असरदार दूसरे साधनों का इंतज़ाम 
किया जाएगा |

सुरक्षित किया जाएगा, जिसे ले जाई जा रही लकड़ी के प्रकार के हिसाब से महानिदेशक मंजूरी दें । 


(7) यात्रा के सभी चरणों में स्थिरता का एक सुरक्षित मार्जिन बनाए रखने का इंतज़ाम किया जाएगा ; इसमें वज़न में 
होने वाली बढ़ोतरी (जैसे पानी सोखने या बर्फ़ जमने से होने वाली बढ़ोतरी, यदि लागू हो) और वज़न में होने वाली कमी 
(जैसे ईंधन और सामान की खपत से होने वाली कमी) को ध्यान में रखा जाएगा |

होगा जो इस प्रकार निर्धारित किया गया है, लेकिन प्रत्येक मामले में उसे उतनी मात्रा से बढ़ाया जाएगा जितनी कि

जहाँ किसी अधिसंरचना विभाजन-पट्‌ट में धँसा हुआ भाग हो, वहाँ अधिसंरचना की प्रभावी लंबाई को उस राशि के बराबर 
घटाया जाएगा, जो शीर्ष दृश्य में अवकाश के क्षेत्रफल को अवकाश की मध्य-लंबाई पर अधिसंरचना की चौड़ाई से विभाजित 
करने पर प्राप्त हो। जहाँ ear हुआ भाग केंद्रीय रेखा के संबंध में असममित हो, वहाँ अवकाश के सबसे बड़े भाग को पोत 
के दोनों पाश्वों पर लागू माना जाएगा। किसी धँसा हुआ भाग पर Soh होना आवश्यक नहीं होगा। 


(ख) जहाँ किसी संलग्न अधिसंरचना का अंतिम विभाजन-पट॒ट, अधिसंरचना के पार्श्वों के साथ अपने प्रतिच्छेदन से आगे 
एक समान उभरा हुआ वक्र में विस्तृत हो, वहाँ अधिसंरचना की लंबाई को समतुल्य समतल्र विभाजन-पट्‌ट के आधार पर 
बढ़ाया जा सकेगा। यह वृद्धि वक्रता के अग्र तथा पश्च विस्तार के दो-तिहाई के बराबर होगी। इस वृद्धि के अवधारण में 
गणना में ली जाने वाली अधिकतम वक्रता, अधिसंरचना के वक्रित सिरे और उसके पार्श्व के प्रतिच्छेदन बिंदु पर अधिसंरचना 
की चौड़ाई के आधे के बराबर होगी।

बढ़ाया जा सकेगा। यह परवल्रय, केंद्रीय रेखा पर विस्तार से प्रारंभ होकर वास्तविक अधिसंरचना विभाजन-पट्ट तथा विस्तार 
के पार््वों के संधि-बिंदु से होकर पोत के पाश्वों तक विस्तृत होगा। यह परवत्रय, अधिसंरचना तथा उसके विस्तारों की सीमा 
के भीतर पूर्णतः अंतर्निहित होगा। 


यदि अधिसंरचना पार्श्व से भीतर की ओर स्थित हो, प्रथम अनुसूची के पैरा i के उप-पैरा अ के खंड (9) के अधीन

(क) जहाँ शीयर प्रोफाइल मानक से भिन्‍न हो, वहाँ अग्र अथवा पश्च अर्धभाग के प्रत्येक प्रोफाइल के चार निर्देशांकों को

मध्य अंतर को 8 से विभाजित करने पर प्राप्त मान, अग्र अथवा पश्च भाग में वक्रता (शीयर) की न्‍यूनता अथवा अधिकता 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 69 


को मापेगा। अग्र तथा पश्च अर्धभागों में अधिकता अथवा नन्‍्यूनता का अंकगणितीय माध्य, वक्रता (शीयर) की अधिकता 
अथवा न्यूनता को मापेगा। 


(ख) जहाँ शीयर प्रोफाइल का पश्च अर्धभाग मानक से अधिक तथा अग्र अर्धभाग मानक से कम हो, वहाँ अधिक भाग के 
लिए कोई लाभ अनुज्ञात नहीं किया जाएगा तथा केवल नन्‍्यूनता का ही मापन किया जाएगा। 


(ग) जहाँ शीयर प्रोफाइल का अग्र अर्धभाग मानक शीयर प्रोफाइल से अधिक हो तथा पश्च अर्धभाग मानक शीयर प्रोफाइल 
का 75 प्रतिशत से कम न हो, वहाँ अधिक भाग के लिए लाभ अनुज्ञात किया जाएगा। जहाँ शीयर प्रोफाइल का पश्च 
अर्धभाग मानक शीयर प्रोफाइल के 50 प्रतिशत से कम हो, वहाँ अग्र भाग में अधिक वक्रता के लिए कोई लाभ नहीं दिया 
जाएगा। जहाँ अर्धभाग में वक्रता (शीयर), मानक शीयर प्रोफाइल के 50 प्रतिशत और 75 प्रतिशत के मध्य हो, वहाँ अग्र 
भाग में अधिक वक्रता के लिए मध्यवर्ती भत्ते दी जा सकेंगी।

तक प्रदान किया जाएगा, ताकि सभी सकारात्मक श्रेणियों पर सही लीवर की सांख्यिकीय स्थिरता

3. दक्षिणी शीतकालीन मौसमी परिक्षेत्र 
(4) दक्षिणी शीतकालीन मौसमी परिक्षेत्र की उत्तरी सीमा है- 


अमेरिकी महाद्वीप के पूर्वी तट पर केप ट्रेस पुंटास से शुरू होकर अक्षांश 34° दक्षिण और देशांतर 50° 
पश्चिम तक जाने वाली सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन), फिर अक्षांश 34° दक्षिण से देशांतर (7° पूर्व तक, फिर 
सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) अक्षांश 35° (0' दक्षिण और देशांतर 20” पूर्व तक, फिर सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) 
अक्षांश 34° दक्षिण और देशांतर 28° पूर्व तक, फिर सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) अक्षांश 35° 30' दक्षिण और 
देशांतर (48° पूर्व तक, फिर सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) अक्षांश तस्मानिया के उत्तर-पश्चिमी तट पर स्थित 
केप ग्रिम तक, फिर तस्मानिया के उत्तरी और पूर्वी तटों के साथ-साथ seit दवीप के सबसे दक्षिणी बिंदु 
तक, फिर सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) अक्षांश स्टीवर्ट दवीप पर स्थित ब्लैक रॉक पॉइंट तक, फिर सीधी रेखा 
(रम्ब लाइन) अक्षांश 47° दक्षिण और देशांतर 70° पूर्व तक, और फिर सीधी रेखा (रम्ब लाइन) के साथ-

4. उष्णकटिबंधीय परिक्षेत्र 

(l) उष्णकटिबंधीय क्षेत्र की उत्तरी सीमा 

उष्णकटिबंधीय क्षेत्र की उत्तरी सीमा है- 

अमेरिकी महादवीप के पूर्वी तट से 60° पश्चिम देशांतर तक फैली (3° उत्तर अक्षांश रेखा का समानांतर। 


फिर वहां से रम्ब रेखा अक्षांश i0°N और देशांतर 58°W तक, फिर अक्षांश i0°N के समानांतर 20°W 
तक, फिर देशांतर 20°W के मध्याहन तक और फिर अक्षांश 30°N के समानांतर अफ्रीका के पश्चिमी तट 
तक, अफ्रीका के पूर्वी तट से अक्षांश 8°N के समानांतर 70°E तक, फिर देशांतर 70°E के मध्याहन तक, 
फिर अक्षांश 73°N तक, फिर अक्षांश i3°N के समानांतर भारत के पश्चिमी तट तक, फिर भारत के

दक्षिण A 40° उत्तर अक्षांश रेखा द्वारा 3° पूर्व देशांतर से सार्डिनिया के पश्चिमी तट तक, पूर्व में सार्डिनिया 
के पश्चिमी और उत्तरी act द्वारा 40° उत्तर अक्षांश से 9° पूर्व देशांतर तक, फिर 9" पूर्व देशांतर रेखा 
द्वारा कोर्सिका के दक्षिणी तट तक, फिर कोर्सिका के पश्चिमी और उत्तरी तटों द्वारा 9° पूर्व देशांतर तक 
और फिर अंगूठे की रेखा द्वारा केप सिसिए तक; यह एक शीतकालीन मौसमी क्षेत्र है।

MINISTRY OF PORTS, SHIPPING AND WATERWAYS 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 25th June, 2026 


G.S.R. 549(E). — In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-sections () and (2) of section 
7/6, sub-section (2) of section 2 read with sub-section (/) and clause (f) of sub-section (2) of 
section 30 of the Merchant Shipping Act, 2025 (24 of 2025), the Central Government hereby 
makes the following rules, namely: — 


PART | 
PRELIMINARY 


i. Short title and commencement. — (i) These rules may be called the Merchant Shipping 
(Load Line) Rules, 2026. 


(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 
2. Application. — (|) These rules shall apply to— 


(a) every Indian vessel, other than a vessel of war, a Government vessel not engaged in 
commercial service, a fishing vessel, a pleasure yacht or a sailing vessel, which is— 


(i) 24 meters or more in length; or 
(ii) 50 gross tonnage or more; or 


(b) every vessel other than Indian vessel to which the International Convention on Load 
Lines, 966, as amended by the Protocol of 988 and subsequent amendments, 
applies, while it is— 


(i) within the coastal waters of India; or 
(ii) at any port or place in India, 


(iii) except when entering such waters or port solely due to stress of 
weather or circumstances beyond the control of the master, owner or 
charterer; or 


(c) Indian vessels not engaged on international voyages but falling within clause (a) shall 
be required to carry an India Load Line Certificate under these rules, while vessels 
engaged on international voyages shall be required to carry an International Load Line 
Certificate, as applicable: 


Provided that the requirements under these rules shall apply only to 
vessels, the keel of which was laid or was at a similar stage of construction 
after the coming into force of these rules: 


Provided further that such vessels, the keel of which was laid or was at a 
similar stage of construction before the date of coming into force of these 


46 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


rules, shall comply with the requirements specified in Merchant Shipping 
(Load Line) Rules, 979 made under the Merchant Shipping Act, 958 (44 of 
958); as it existed prior to its repeal 


(2) every vessel to which these rules apply under this rule shall comply with the 
requirements of the International Convention on Load Lines, 966, as amended, as 
implemented through these rules and the Schedules. 


(3) Every vessel to which these rules apply under this rule shall comply with the technical 
requirements specified in the Schedules to these rules, subject to the part-wise 
applicability indicated therein. 


3. Definitions. — (/) In these rules, unless the context otherwise requires,— 
(a) "Act" means the Merchant Shipping Act, 2025 (24 of 2025); 
(b) "approved" means approved by the assigning authority; 


(c) "assigning Authority" means the Chief Ship Surveyor, or any other surveyor authorised 
in this behalf by the Director-General, who shall assign the load line in accordance 
with section 2 of the Act; 


(d) “Convention” means the International Convention on Load Lines, 966, as amended 
by the Protocol of 988, and as amended; 


(e) "existing vessel " means a vessel which is not a new vessel; 


(f) “Load Line Certificate” means an International Load Line Certificate or India Load Line 
Certificate, as the case may be, as issued under rule 8; 


(g) "new ship" means a ship the keel of which is laid, or which is at a similar stage of 
construction, on or after the date of coming into force of these rules 


(h) "notice” means any notice issued by the Director-General under section 30/ of the Act; 
(i) “Schedule” means a Schedule annexed to these rules; 
(j) “similar stage of construction”, in relation to a vessel, means the stage at which— 

(i) construction identifiable with a specific vessel begins; and 


(ii) assembly of that vessel has commenced comprising at least 50 
tonnes or / per cent. of the estimated mass of all structural material, 
whichever is less. 


(2) Words and expressions used in these rules but not defined herein shall have the 
meanings respectively assigned to them in the Act, and in the applicable Codes 
and Conventions. 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 47 


PART Il 
SURVEYS AND CERTIFICATES 


4. Application for Survey. — () Application for the survey of a vessel for the purpose of 
assignment of freeboard and for the issue of a load line certificate shall be made to the 
Assigning Authority by or on behalf of the owner of the vessel. 


(2) The applicant shall furnish to the Assigning Authority such plans, drawings and 
specifications including— 


(a) the general arrangement plan; 
(b) — hull construction and deck layout; 
(c) stability information; and 

(d) load line particulars —, 


and other documents and information relating to the design and construction of the 
vessel as the Assigning Authority may require. 


5. Load line surveys. — () Upon receipt of the application and the documents together with 
other relevant information required under rule 4, the Assigning Authority shall cause the vessel 
to be surveyed, including, in the case of a vessel being put into service for the first time, an 
initial survey consisting of a complete inspection of its structure, equipment, arrangements, 
material and scantlings so as to ensure compliance with these rules and the Convention, for 
the purpose of determining— 


(a) whether the vessel complies with such of the requirements of Part IV of these rules 
and the First Schedule, as applicable; and 


(b) such other data as may be necessary:- 


(i) for the assignment of freeboard to the vessel in accordance with Part V of 
these rules and the Second Schedule; and 


(ii) to enable the stability information to be supplied to the master of the vessel 
pursuant to Part VI of these rules and the Third Schedule. 


(2) In the course of survey of a vessel pursuant to the provisions of sub-rule 5, the vessel 
and any of its fittings shall be submitted to such tests as may, in the opinion of the 
Assigning Authority, be necessary to ascertain that the vessel complies with the 
requirements of sub-rule () and any test pertaining to stability of a vessel shall be 
subject to the requirements of rule 39. 


(3) The owner or any other person on his behalf applying for the survey of the vessel 
under rule 4, shall afford all necessary facilities for such survey and shall, at the 
request of the Assigning Authority, furnish, for the use of the Assigning Authority and 
retention if necessary, such further documents and information as the Assigning 
Authority may require. 


48 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


6. Report of survey. — () On completion of the survey, the surveyor shall submit a report of 
survey to the Assigning Authority giving the results of the survey and his findings on the 
condition of the vessel with reference to the requirements of rule Error! Reference source 
not found.. 


(2) The report of survey shall be accompanied by the record of conditions of assignment 
in the form set out in the Fourth Schedule complying with the requirements of rule 34 
and the computations of freeboard complying with the requirement of Part V of these 
rules and the Second Schedule. 


(3) In the case of any vessel which is required to comply with the requirements of Part | 
of the Third Schedule relating to stability the surveyor shall furnish to the Assigning 
Authority such information as may be necessary to determine whether the vessel 
complies with these requirements. 


7. Assignment of freeboards. — (7) The Assigning Authority shall— 


(a) if satisfied on scrutiny of report of survey that vessel complies with the applicable 
requirements of Part IV of these rules and the First Schedule; and 


(0) on receipt of an intimation from the Assigning Authority that it is satisfied that the vessel 
complies with the requirements of Part VI of these rules and the Third Schedule 
relating to stability, 


assign freeboard to the vessel in accordance with Part V of these rules and Second 
Schedule. 

(2) On assignment of freeboards, the Assigning Authority shall furnish to the owner of 
the vessel— 


(a) particulars of freeboards so assigned; 
(0) directions specifying— 


(i) the load lines to be marked on the vessel in accordance with the 
requirements of Part Ill of these rules; 


(ii) the position in which those load lines, the deck line and the load line 
mark are to be so marked; and 


(c) two copies of the Record of Conditions of Assignment. 


8. Issue of Load Line Certificates and Forms thereof. — (/) Subject to the provisions of rule 
73, the Assigning Authority shall, on being satisfied that the vessel has been duly marked in 
accordance with the directions given to the owner of the vessel under rule 7, issue either an 
International Load Line Certificate (966) or an India Load Line Certificate as may be required 
under the Act, in the Form set out for such certificates in the Fifth Schedule. 


(2) Certificates may be issued by another government in the following manner, 
namely:— 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 49 


(a) a contracting government may, at the request of the Director-General, cause a vessel 
to be surveyed and, if satisfied that the provisions of the the Convention as amended 
are complied with, shall issue or authorise the issue of an International Load Line 
Certificate to the vessel in accordance with the International Load Line Convention 
966, as amended by its 988 Protocol; 


Ss 


a copy of the said certificate, a copy of the survey report used for computing the 
freeboard, and a copy of the computations shall be transmitted as early as possible to 
the Director-General; and 


a certificate so issued shall contain a statement to the effect that it has been issued at 
the request of the Government of India, whose flag the vessel is or will be flying and, 
it shall have the same force and receive the same recognition as a certificate issued 
under these rules.

(2 
na 


(3) Multiple load lines may be assigned in the following circumstances and manner, 
namely:— 


(a) where a vessel is intended to operate under different service conditions, seasonal 
zones or trading patterns, the Director-General may assign more than one load line to 
such vessel, subject to compliance with these rules; and 


(b) the procedure, conditions and limitations for the assignment of multiple load lines for 
Indian vessels shall be in accordance with such instructions or notices as may be 
issued by the Director-General from time to time. 


9. Duration of certificate. — Every Load Line Certificate issued under these rules shall be valid 
until a date to be determined by the Assigning Authority not being a date more than five years 
after the date of completion of survey of the vessel under rule Error! Reference source not 
found. and such date shall be specified by the Assigning Authority in every certificate issued 
by it, failing which the certificate shall not be deemed to be a valid certificate. 


0. Extension of Load Line Certificate. — () Where any vessel, in respect of which a Load 
Line Certificate is in force, has been surveyed following an application made by the owner for 
the issue of a fresh certificate to take effect on the expiry of the current certificate, the 
Assigning Authority or surveyor may,— 


(a) if satisfied, on scrutiny of survey, that the vessel complies with applicable requirements 
of Part IV of these rules and of the First Schedule relating to conditions of assignment; 
or 


(b) on receipt of an intimation from the Director-General that the vessel complies with the 
requirements of the Third Schedule relating to stability; 


(c) extend the validity of the current certificates of the vessel by a period not exceeding 
five months, if he considers that it is not reasonably practicable to issue a fresh 


50 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


certificate and in every such case, a fresh certificate shall be issued after the expiry of 
the extended date of validity of the current certificate. 


(2) No such extension granted under sub-rule (4) shall have effect unless particulars of 
the date upto which the period of validity of the certificate is extended, together with 
particulars of place at and date on which extension was granted, are endorsed by 
the Assigning Authority or surveyor, on the current certificate. 


(3) The period of validity of a fresh certificate ultimately issued to a vessel under sub- 
rule () shall not exceed five years from the date of completion of survey referred to 
in the said sub-rule. 


. Cancellation of Load Line Certificates. — (i) Where the Director-General is satisfied, 
whether by a report from an Assigning Authority or a surveyor or otherwise, that — 


(a) the vessel to which the certificate relates does not comply with the conditions of 
assignment; or 


(b) the structural strength of the vessel is lowered to such an extent as to render the vessel 
unsafe; or 


(c) information on the basis of which freeboards were assigned to the vessel has proved 
to be incorrect in matters of material importance; or 


(0) the fittings and appliances mentioned in clause (a) of sub-rule (4) of rule 72 are not 
maintained in an effective condition, the Director-General may cancel, or cause to be 
cancelled, any Load Line Certificate issued under these rules. 


(2) Where it comes to the notice of the Director-General that— 
(a) the certificate of a vessel is not endorsed as required by rule 2; or 
(b) anew certificate is issued in respect of the vessel; or 
(c) the vessel has ceased to be an Indian vessel within the meaning of the Act, 


he, after giving the owner of the vessel a reasonable opportunity of making his 
representation, may cancel or cause to cancel any Load Line Certificate issued 
under these rules. 


2. Periodical inspection of vessels. — (i) Every vessel in respect of which a Load Line 
Certificate is in force shall be periodically inspected by a surveyor in accordance with the 
provisions of this rule in order to ensure that— 


(a) the fittings and appliances for the protection of openings, the guard rails, the freeing 
ports and the means of access to quarters of the crew in the vessel are in an effective 
condition; and 


(b) no changes have been made or taken place in the hull or superstructures of the vessel 
which may render any inaccurate data on the basis of which freeboards have been 
assigned to the vessel. 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 5 


(2) Every application for the periodical inspection shall be made by or on behalf of the 
owner of the vessel to the Assigning Authority, who shall appoint a surveyor to carry 
out the inspection. 


(3) The surveyor may, in the course of any such inspection, require such tests to be 
carried out as in his opinion, may be necessary to establish that the vessel complies 
with the requirements of sub-rule (7). 


(4) Periodical inspections required under this rule shall be carried out annually within a 
period of nine to fifteen months commencing on the date three months before and 
concluding three months after, each anniversary of the date of completion of the 
survey of the vessel leading to the issue of its current Load Line Certificate: 


Provided that the Director-General may, if satisfied that the circumstances 
so require, permit the annual inspection to be carried out before or after the 
period specified therefor. 


(5) The surveyor, if satisfied after inspection that the vessel complies with the 
requirements of sub-rule (), shall, in the space provided for this purpose in the Load 
Line Certificates, endorse a record of such inspection and certify— 


(a) in the case of an International Load Line Certificate (966), that the vessel was found 
to comply with the relevant provisions of the Convention; and 


(b) in the case of an India Load Line Certificate, that the vessel was found to comply with 
the relevant provisions of these rules. 
(6) Every such endorsement shall be dated and signed by the surveyor carrying out the 
inspection, specifying the Assigning Authority on whose behalf the inspection was 
carried out. 


3. Exemptions. — () Neighbouring ports exemption: The Director-General may exempt any 
vessel engaged solely on international voyages between near neighbouring ports of two or 
more States from the provisions of these rules and the Convention, provided that— 


(a) the governments of the States in which such ports are situated are satisfied that 
the sheltered nature or conditions of such voyages make it unreasonable or 
impracticable to apply the provisions of the Convention; and 


(0) the vessel remains engaged solely on such voyages. 


(2) Novel vessel or experimental features exemption: The Director-General may 
exempt any vessel which embodies features of a novel kind from any requirement of 
these rules or the Convention, where the application of such requirement would 
seriously impede research into the development of such features: 


Provided that— 


(a) the Director-General is satisfied that the vessel complies with safety 
requirements adequate for the service for which it is intended; and 


52 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


(b) such requirements ensure an overall level of safety not less than that provided under 
the Convention; and 


(c) the exemption is acceptable to the Governments of the States intended to be visited 
by the vessel. 

(3) Single international voyage exemption: A vessel not normally engaged on 
international voyages may, in exceptional circumstances, be exempted by the 
Director-General from any requirement of these rules or the Convention for the 
purpose of undertaking a single international voyage, provided the vessel complies 
with safety requirements adequate for that specific voyage. 


(4) Notification to the International Maritime Organisation: The Director-General 
shall communicate to the International Maritime Organisation the particulars of every 
exemption granted under sub-rules (4), (2), or (3), together with the reasons for 
granting such exemption, for circulation to contracting governments. 


(5) Conditions of exemption: Any exemption granted under this rule— 


(a) shall be subject to such conditions as the Director-General may specify; 
(0) may be limited in duration or voyage; and 


(c) may be modified or cancelled if the Director-General is satisfied that the 
conditions justifying the exemption no longer exist. 


4. Exemption certificate. — (i) Where the Director-General exempts any vessel pursuant to 
the provisions of the Act, the Director-General shall issue in respect of such vessel an 
International Load Line Exemption Certificate in the form set out in the Fifth Schedule. 


(2) Save in so far as the nature and terms of any such exemption require to the contrary 
the provisions of rules 2 to 7 and of rules 9 to 2 shall apply to any vessel so 
exempted and to any exemption certificate so issued to any such vessel in the like 
manner as they apply to any other vessel, except that— 


(a) the references in the said rules to Assigning Authority shall be deemed to be 
references to the Director-General; and 


(b) the surveyor, if satisfied after inspection, that the vessel continues to comply with the 
conditions subject to which the exemption was granted, shall endorse the exemption 
certificate to that effect in the space provided and date and sign the endorsement. 


5. Equivalents. — () The Director-General may permit any fitting, material, appliance or 
apparatus to be fitted, or any other provision to be adopted on a vessel, in lieu of that required 
by these rules or by the Convention, if satisfied, whether by trial or otherwise, that such fitting, 
material, appliance, apparatus or provision is at least as effective as the specified requirement. 


(2) Where any such equivalent is permitted under sub-rule (), the Director-General may 
impose such conditions as he considers necessary for ensuring an equivalent level 
of safety. 


(3) The Director-General shall communicate to the International Maritime Organisation, 
for circulation to the contracting governments, the particulars of any equivalent 
permitted under this rule, together with a report of any trials conducted in respect 
thereof. 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 53 


6. 


7. 


8. 


9. 


Approvals for experimental purposes. — (i) The Director-General may grant specific 
approvals for experimental purposes in respect of any vessel to which the Convention applies, 
where such approval is considered necessary to facilitate the development of innovative 
designs, arrangements, or safety features. 


(2) Any approval granted under sub-rule (7) shall be subject to such conditions as the 
Director-General may specify, to ensure that the overall level of safety of the vessel 
is not reduced. 


(3) The Director-General shall communicate to the International Maritime Organisation, 
for circulation to the contracting governments, the particulars of every approval 
granted under this rule. 


Repairs, alterations and modifications. — (/) A vessel that undergoes repairs, alterations, 
modifications, or any related outfitting shall continue to comply with at least the requirements 
previously applicable to that vessel under these rules and under the Convention and an 
existing vessel shall not, as a rule, comply to a lesser extent with the requirements for a new 
vessel than it did before such repairs, alterations or modifications. 


(2) Repairs, alterations and modifications of a major character, and any related outfitting, 
shall comply with the requirements applicable to a new vessel, insofar as the 
Director-General considers reasonable and practicable, having regard to the extent 
and nature of such work. 


Zones and areas. — () Every vessel to which these rules apply shall comply with the 
requirements relating to load lines and seasonal zones applicable to that vessel in the zones 
and areas specified in the Sixth Schedule, being the zones and areas corresponding to Annex 
Il of the Convention. 


(2) For the purposes of these rules, where a port stands on the boundary line between 
two zones or areas specified in the Sixth Schedule, such port shall be regarded as 
lying within the zone or area from or into which the vessel arrives or departs. 


Submersion. — (/) Except as provided in sub-rules (2) and (3), a vessel to which these 
rules apply shall ensure that the appropriate load line corresponding to the season of the year 
and the zone or area specified in the Sixth Schedule is not submerged at any time when the 
vessel puts to sea, during the voyage, or on arrival. 


(2) When a vessel is floating in fresh water of unit density (7.000), the applicable load 
line may be submerged by the amount of the freshwater allowance shown on the 
vessel’s Load Line Certificate and where the actual water density differs from unity, 
an allowance shall be made proportional to the difference between 7.025 and the 
actual density. 


(3) When a vessel departs from a port situated on a river or inland waters, deeper loading 
may be permitted corresponding to the weight of fuel, water and other consumable 
materials required to be expended between the point of departure and the open sea. 


(4) Nothing in this rule shall permit a vessel to exceed the freeboards assigned by the 
Assigning Authority under the Act, except as expressly provided by the fresh water 
allowance or the allowances in sub-rule (3). 


54 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


20. Port State Control. — (/) A vessel other than an Indian Vessel holding a valid International 
Load Line Certificate or International Load Line Exemption Certificate issued under the 
Convention shall, when such a vessel is within any port or place in India, be subject to port 
state control, which shall be limited to verifying that— 


(a) the vessel is not loaded beyond the limits permitted by the certificate; 
(b) the actual load line marks correspond with the certificate; and 


(c) the vessel has not been so materially altered in respect of these matters that it is 
manifestly unfit to proceed to sea without danger to human life. 


(2) Where a vessel holds a valid International Load Line Exemption Certificate, control 
exercised under sub-rule () shall be limited to verifying compliance with the 
conditions stated in that certificate. 


(3) Control under this rule shall be exercised only to the extent necessary to ensure that 
the vessel does not sail until it can safely proceed to sea without danger to 
passengers or crew. 


(4) Where control under this rule results in any intervention, the authorised officer shall 
immediately inform, in writing, the Consular or diplomatic representative of the State 
whose flag the vessel is entitled to fly, stating the reasons for the intervention and all 
relevant circumstances. 


(5) Nothing in this rule shall affect the powers exercisable by the Director-General or an 
authorised officer under the Act in respect of vessels calling at Indian ports. 


PART Ill 
LOAD LINES AND MARKS 


2. Appropriate marks. — For the purposes of this part, the expression "appropriate marks" in 
relation to a vessel means the load lines required to be marked on the vessel under clause 
(0) of sub-rule (2) of rule 7 and the deck-line and load line mark. 


22. Marking of load lines. — The owner of a vessel, on receipt from the Assigning Authority of 
particulars and directions referred to in sub-rule (2) of rule 7, shall cause the appropriate marks 
to be marked on each side of the vessel in accordance with the said directions and 
requirements of this Part. 


23. Deck-line. — () The deck-line shall consist of a horizontal line 300 millimetres in length and 
25 millimetres in width, which shall be marked amidships on each side of the vessel in 
accordance with the provisions of this rule so as to indicate the position of the freeboard deck.

(2) Subject to the provisions of sub-rule (3), the deck-line shall be marked in such a 
position on the side of the vessel that its upper edge passes through the point 
amidships, where the continuation outward-- 


(a) the upper surface of the freeboard deck; or 
(b) any sheathing of the freeboard deck, 
intersects the outer surface of the shell of the vessel as shown in Figure . 


(3) Where, in the opinion of the Assigning Authority, the design of the vessel or any 
other circumstance renders it impracticable to mark the deck-line in accordance 
with the provisions of sub-rule (2), the Assigning Authority may, in the directions 
given under sub-rule (2) of rule 7 include a direction that the deck-line may be 
marked by reference to another point on the side of the vessel which is as near as 
practicable to the position referred to in sub-rule (2) and the location of the 
reference point and the identification of the freeboard deck shall in all cases be 
indicated on the International Load Line Certificate. 


24. Load line mark. — The load line mark shall consist, as shown in Figure 2, of a ring 300 
millimetres in outside diameter and 25 millimetres wide, intersected by a horizontal line 450 
millimetres long and 25 millimetres wide the upper edge of which passes through the center 
of the ring, which shall be marked amidships vertically below the deck-line, so that except as 
otherwise provided in rule 37, the distance from the center of the ring to the upper edge of the 
deck-line is equal to the summer freeboard assigned to the vessel.

25. Load lines. — (4) Load lines as specified in sub-rule (2) and rule 26 shall be deemed to 
indicate the maximum depth to which a vessel marked therewith may be loaded in the 
circumstances specified in the Sixth Schedule in relation to appropriate load lines, zones, 
areas and seasonal periods. 

(2) Except as otherwise provided in rule 26 and rule 37, load lines shall consist as 
shown in Figure 2 of horizontal lines each 230 milimetres in length and 25 
millimetres in width extending forward or abaft of a vertical line 25 millimetres in 
width marked 540 millimetres forward of the center of the ring of the load line mark 
and at right angles to that line. 

(3) The individual load lines shall be— 


(a) the summer load line, which shall extend forward of the aforesaid vertical line and the 
marked "S", which shall correspond horizontally with the line passing through the 
center of the ring of the load line mark; 


(0) the winter load line, which shall extend forward of the aforesaid vertical line and be 
marked "W"; 


(c) the winter North Atlantic load line, which shall extend forward of the aforesaid vertical 
line and be marked "WNA"; 


(d) the tropical load line, which shall extend forward of the aforesaid vertical line and be 
marked "T"; 


(e) the fresh water load line, which shall extend abaft the aforesaid vertical line and be 
marked "F"; and 


(f) the tropical fresh Water load line, which shall extend abaft the aforesaid vertical line 
and be marked "TF"; 
(4) The maximum depth of loading referred to in sub-rule () shall be the depth 
indicated by the upper edge of the appropriate load line specified in sub-rule (2). 


26. Timber load lines. — (/) Timber load lines, as shown in Figure 3, shall consist of horizontal 
lines of the dimensions specified in respect of such lines in rule 25, extending abaft or forward 
of a vertical line of the dimensions specified in respect of such a line in that rule, marked 540 
millimetres abaft the center of the ring of load line mark and at right angles of that line. 


(2) Individual timber load lines shall be as follows:-

(a) the summer Timber Load Line, which shall extend abaft the said vertical line and be 
marked LS; 


(b) the winter Load Line, which shall extend abaft the said vertical line and be marked LW; 


(c) the winter North Atlantic Timber Load Line which shall extend abaft the said vertical 
line and be marked LWNA; 


(d) the tropical timber Load Line, which shall extend abaft the said vertical line and be 
marked LT ; 


(e) the fresh water timber Load Line, which shall extend forward of the said vertical line 
and be marked LTF LF; and 


(f) the tropical fresh water timber Load Line, which shall extend forward of the said vertical 
line and be marked LLF LTF; and 


(3) The maximum depth of loading referred to in sub-rule () of rule 25 shall be the 
depth indicated by the upper edge of the appropriate timber load line. 


27. Appropriate load line. — The appropriate load line in respect of a vessel at any particular 
place and time shall be ascertained in accordance with the provisions of the Sixth Schedule. 


28. Position of load lines. — Each load line required to be marked on a vessel, shall be marked 
in such a position of each side of the vessel such that the distance measured vertically 
downwards from the upper edge of the deck-line to the upper edge of the load line is equal to 
the freeboard assigned to the vessel which is appropriate to that load line. 


29. Methods of marking. — (i) The appropriate marks shall be marked on each side of the 
vessel in accordance with the requirements of sub-rules (2) and (3) in such a manner as to 
be plainly visible. 


(2) If the sides of the vessel are of — 
(a) metal, the appropriate marks shall be cut in, center punched or welded; 
(0) wood, the marks shall be cut into the planking to a depth of not less than 3 millimetres; 


58 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


(c) any other materials to which the foregoing method of marking cannot be effectively 
applied, the marks shall be permanently affixed to the sides of the vessel by bonding 
or some other effective method. 

(3) The appropriate marks shall be painted in white or yellow, if the background is 
dark, and in black if the background is light. 


30. Authorisation of removal etc. of appropriate marks. — After the appropriate marks have 
been marked on a vessel, such marks shall not be concealed, defaced or obliterated and shall 
not be removed or altered, except with the prior approval of the Assigning Authority. 


3. Mark of Assigning Authority. — (i) The mark of the Assigning Authority specified in sub- 
rule (2) shall be marked on each side of the vessel in a position alongside the load line mark, 
either above the horizontal line forming part of that mark or above and below it. 


(2) The Assigning Authority's mark shall consist of not more than four initials to identify 
their name, each measuring approximately 45 millimetres in height and 75 
millimetres in width. 


PART IV 
CONDITIONS OF ASSIGNMENT 


32. Assignment of freeboards. — (!) Except as otherwise provided in sub-rules (2) and (3), 
every vessel to which freeboards are to be assigned under these rules, shall comply with the 
requirements applicable to it under Part | of the First Schedule. 


(2) Every vessel,— 
(a) being a vessel of Type 'A’, to which requirements of Part Ill of the First Schedule apply; 


(b) being a vessel of Type 'B', to which the requirements of Part IV of the First Schedule 
apply; or 

(c) being a vessel to be assigned with timber freeboards, to which the requirements of 
Part V of the First Schedule apply; 


shall comply with the requirements of the respective Part of the First Schedule and 
also the requirements of Part Il of the said Schedule, except in so far as the 
compliance of Part Ill, |V or V, as the case may be, of the said Schedule may 
otherwise require. 


(3) Every existing vessel, not being a vessel to which freeboards are required to be 
assigned in accordance with sub-rule () of rule 36 read with the proviso to sub-rule 
(2) thereof, shall comply with such of the requirements relevant to the assignment of 
freeboards to the vessel, as were applicable to it under the law in force immediately 
prior to the coming into force of these rules. 


33. Compliance with conditions of assignment. — (/) Except as otherwise provided in sub- 
rule (2), a vessel shall be deemed to be not complying with the conditions of assignment,— 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 59 


(a) if at any time after the assignment of freeboards to it, there has been any alteration of 
the hull superstructures, fitting or appliances of the vessel to such extent that either— 


(i) any requirement applicable to the vessel under rule 32 is not complied with; 
or 


(ii) the record of conditions of assignment made in relation to the vessel 
pursuant to rule 34 is rendered inaccurate in a material respect ; or 


(b) if the record of conditions of assignment is not kept on board the vessel in accordance 
with sub-rule (3) of rule 34. 


(2) Irrespective of any alteration in the vessel as specified in clause (a) of sub-rule (), a 
vessel shall be deemed to be complying with the conditions of assignment, if either— 


(a) fresh freeboards appropriate to the condition of the vessel after the alterations have 
been assigned to it and it has been marked with load lines and a fresh certificate has 
been issued to its owner; or 


(b) the alteration has been inspected by a surveyor on behalf of the Assigning Authority, 
and the Assigning Authority is satisfied that the alteration is not such as to require any 
change in the freeboards assigned to the vessel and full particulars of the alteration 
together with date and place of inspection have been endorsed by the surveyor on the 
record of conditions of assignment. 


34. Record of conditions of assignment. — (!) The record of conditions of assignment in 
respect of the hull, superstructure fittings and appliances of a vessel to which freeboards are 
assigned shall be in the form set out in the Fourth Schedule, or a Form as near thereto as 
circumstances permit, and shall contain the particulars required by that Form and such 
particulars may be furnished by attaching to the record a copy of the report of survey and 
specifying in the record passages from that report in which the relevant particulars are given. 


(2) The record shall be completed by the surveyor carrying out the survey of the vessel 
pursuant to rule 5 and shall be furnished by him to the Assigning Authority in 
accordance with the provisions of rule 6 and two copies of the record shall be sent 
by the Assigning Authority to the owner of the vessel together with the particulars 
and directions required to be so furnished by rule 7. 


(3) One copy of the record of conditions of assignment, particulars and directions 
furnished by the Assigning Authority to the owner of the vessel shall, at all times 
be kept on board the vessel in the custody of the master of the vessel. 


PART V 
FREE BOARDS 
35. Types of freeboards . — The freeboards assignable to any vessel under the rules shall be— 
() summer freeboard; 


(2) tropical freeboard; 


60 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


3) winter freeboard; 
4) winter north atlantic freeboard; 
5) fresh water freeboard; 


6) tropical fresh water freeboard; 


(3) 
(4) 
(5) 
(6) 
(7) summer timber freeboard; 
(8) winter timber freeboard; 
(9) winter north atlantic timber freeboard; 
(0) tropical timber freeboard; 
() fresh water timber freeboard; and 
(2) tropical fresh water timber freeboard. 
36. Determination of freeboards. — Except as otherwise provided in rule 37, 


(a) the freeboards to be assigned to a new vessel shall be determined in accordance with 
the provisions of the Second Schedule; and 


(b) the freeboards to be assigned to an existing vessel shall be determined in 
accordance with the provisions applicable in that behalf to the vessel under the rules 
in force immediately prior to the coming into force of these rules : 


Provided that if an existing vessel has been so constructed or altered so as 
to comply with the requirements of the First Schedule applicable to a new 
vessel of its type, and an application is made in respect of such vessel for 
the assignment of freeboards determined in accordance with the provisions 
of the Second Schedule, such freeboards may be assigned to the vessel. 


37. Exceptions regarding freeboards. — () Greater than minimum freeboard: Any vessel may, 
on the application of the owner made in that behalf be assigned greater than the minimum 
freeboard determinable in accordance with rule 36, subject to the following conditions, 
namely— 


(a) on survey of the vessel pursuant to rule 5, the Assigning Authority is satisfied that the 
vessel complies with the requirements of-- 


(i) Part IV of these rules ; 
(ii) the First Schedule, other than those relating to stability; 
(iii) Part VI of these rules in so far as they relate to stability; and 
(iv) the Third Schedule in so far as they relate to stability; 
(b) the vessel is not assigned with timber freeboards; 


(c) if the greater than minimum freeboard to be assigned to the vessel is such that the 
position of load lines on the sides of the vessel appropriate to that freeboard would 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 6 


correspond to, or be lower than, the position at which the lowest of the load lines 
appropriate to minimum freeboards for that vessel would have been marked,— 


(i) the load line appropriate to the greater than minimum freeboard and the 
fresh water freeboard shall only be marked on the sides of the vessel; 


(ii) the load line appropriate to the greater than minimum freeboard shall be 
called the "All season load line" which shall consist of horizontal line 
intersecting the load line mark and such mark shall be placed 
accordingly; 


(iii) the vertical line specified in the relevant marking provisions in sub-rule 
(2) of rule 25 shall be omitted; and 


(iv) subject to sub-clause (iii), the fresh water load line shall be specified in 
sub-rule (2) of rule 25 and shall be marked accordingly. 


(2) Lesser than minimum freeboard: On an application made in this behalf by the owner 
of a hopper type vessel, which is engaged on voyages other than international 
voyages during the course of which it does not go farther than 20 miles from the 
nearest land at any time, the Director-General may, subject to the conditions set out 
in Part IV of the Second Schedule, assign such vessel lesser than minimum 
freeboard reduced to— 


(a) five-eighth of the appropriate minimum freeboard determinable in accordance with the 
Table B set out in Part V of the Second Schedule ; or 


(b) one-half of the appropriate minimum freeboard determinable in accordance with sub- 
paragraph (5) or sub-paragraph (6) of paragraph 978 of the Second Schedule : 


Provided that such freeboard shall not in either case be less than 50 millimetres. 


38. Special position of deck-line and correction of free-board. — In any case, where the deck- 
line is to be marked on the sides of a vessel in accordance with sub-rule (3) of rule 23, the 
freeboards to be assigned to the vessel shall be corrected to allow for the vertical distance by 
which the position of the deck-line is altered by virtue of that sub-rule and the location of the 
point by reference to which the deck-line has been so marked and the identity of the deck 
which has been regarded as the freeboard deck, shall be specified in the Load Line Certificate 
issued in respect of such vessel. 


PART VI 
STABILITY AND LOADING 


39. Information as to stability of vessels. — (7) The owner of every vessel to which freeboards 
are assigned under these rules shall, for the guidance of the master of the vessel, provide 
information relating to the stability of the vessel in accordance with the provisions of this rule. 


62 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


(2) The information under sub-rule () shall include stability particulars appropriate to 
the vessel in respect of all matters specified in Part Il of the Third Schedule and the 
method of computation and the form of the particulars shall, so far as practicable, be 
be in accordance with Part Ill of that Schedule or equivalent thereto. 


(3) The stability characteristics shall comply with the criteria specified in Part | of the 
Third Schedule and the International Code on Intact Stability, 2008 (IS Code 2008), 
as amended from time to time. 


(4) Subject to the provisions of sub-rule (5), the information shall, when first supplied, be 
based on the determination of stability by means of an inclining test which shall, 
unless the Assigning Authority otherwise permits, be carried out in the presence of a 
surveyor appointed by the Director-General and the information first supplied shall 
be replaced by fresh information whenever its accuracy is materially affected by 
alteration of the vessel, which shall, if the Director-General so requires be based on 
a further inclining test. 


(5) The Assigning Authority may--- 


(a) in the case of any vessel, permit the information to be based on the determination of 
stability of sister vessel by means of an inclining test; or 


(b) in the case of any vessel specially designed for the carriage of liquids or ore in bulk, 
dispense with the inclining test if from the information available in respect of similar 
vessels, the Director-General is satisfied that the vessel proportions and arrangements 
are such as to ensure adequate stability in all probable loading conditions. 


(6) The information, and any fresh information to replace the same pursuant to the 
provisions of sub-rule (4), shall, before issue to the master, be submitted by or on 
behalf of the owner to Assigning Authority for his approval, together with a copy 
thereof for his record, and shall incorporate such additions and amendments, as the 
Director-General may in any particular case require. 


(7) Information provided pursuant to this rule shall be furnished by the owner of the 
vessel to the master in the form of a book, which shall be kept on board the vessel 
at all times in the custody of the master of the vessel. 


(8) Where any alterations are made to a vessel so as to materially affect the loading or 
stability information supplied to the master, amended information shall be provided, 
and if necessary, the vessel shall be re-inclined. 


40. Information as to loading and ballasting of vessels. — ({) The owner of any vessel, being 
a vessel of more than 50 metres in length specially designed to carry liquids or ore in bulk, 
to which freeboards are assigned under these rules shall, for the information of the master of 
the vessel, provide information relating to the loading and ballasting of the vessel in 
accordance with the provisions of sub-rules (2) and (3). 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 63 


(2) The information under sub-rule (7) shall consist of working instructions specifying in 
detail the manner in which the vessel is to be loaded and ballasted, so as to avoid 
the creation of Unacceptable stresses in its structure and shall indicate the maximum 
maximum stresses permissible for the vessel. 


(3) The provisions of sub-rule (6) of rule 39 shall apply in the like manner to the 
information required under sub-rule () and the information duly approved by the 
Assigning Authority shall be contained in the book to be furnished to the master of 
the vessel pursuant to the provisions of sub-rule (7) of rule 39, so however, that 
information required by rule 39 and this rule shall be shown separately in the book 
under separate headings specifying the number and heading of each rule. 


64 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


FIRST SCHEDULE

PART | 
GENERAL 
7. Definitions and Interpretation. — 
A. Definitions: () "amidship" means at the middle of the length (L); 


(2) "block co-efficient" or the symbol (७७) means block co-efficient obtained by the 
formula 


Cp = A/ (L.B.ds) 
where, A is volume of the moulded displacement of the vessel, excluding bossing, in 
a vessel with metal shell or, as the case may be, the volume of displacement to the 
outer surface of the hull in a vessel with a shell of any other material, both taken at a 
moulded draught of d: ; di is 85 per cent of the least moulded depth; 
When calculating the block coefficient of a multi-hull craft, the full breadth (B) as 
defined in sub-paragraph (3) is to be used and not the breadth of a single hull; 

(3) "breadth" or the symbol (B) means the maximum breadth of the vessel, measured 
amidships to the moulded line of the frame in a vessel with a metal shell, or as the 
case may be, to the outer surface of the hull in a vessel with a shell of any other 
material; 


(4) “bridge” is a superstructure which does not extend to either the forward or after 
perpendicular; 


(5) “depth for freeboard" or the symbol (0) - 


(a) in relation to a vessel other than these referred to in clause (b) means the moulded 
depth of the vessel amidships plus the thickness of the free board deck 
stringer plate, where fitted, and 


(0) in relation to a vessel having a rounded gunwale with a radius greater than four per 
cent... of the breadth of the vessel (B), or in relation to a vessel having topsides of 
unusual form means: the depth for freeboard of a vessel having midship section with 
vertical topsides and with the same round of beam and area of top side section equal 
to that provided by the actual midship section; 


(6) "enclosed superstructure" means a superstructure with enclosing bulk heads of 
efficient construction, access openings in such bulkheads being fitted with ails and 
watertight doors and all other openings in sides and ends of such superstructures 
being fitted with efficient watertight means of closing but does not include a bridge or 
a poop fulfilling these requirements unless access is provided by which the crew can 
reach machinery and other working spaces within the bridge or poop by alternative 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 65 


means which are available for the purpose at all times when access openings in the 
bulkheads of the bridge or poop are closed; 


(7) "flush deck vessel " means a vessel which has no superstructures on a free board 
deck; 


(8) "freeboard" means the distance measured vertically downwards amidships from the 
upper edge of the deckline described in rule 23 to the position at which the upper 
edge of the appropriate load line mark lies; 


(9) "freeboard deck" means the deck from which the freeboard is assigned being either— 


(a) the uppermost complete deck exposed to whether and sea, which has permanent 
means of closing all openings in the weather part thereof and below which all openings 
in the sides of the vessel are fitted with permanent means of watertight closing. Ina 
vessel having a discontinuous freeboard deck, the lowest line of the exposed deck and 
the continuation of that line parallel to the upper part of the deck is taken as the 
freeboard deck; or 


(b) on the application of the owner and subject to the approval of the Director General, a 
deck lower than that described in clause (a), provided it is a complete and permanent 
deck continuous in the fore and aft direction at least between the machinery space and 
peak bulkheads and continuous athwartships; and 


(i) when a lower deck is stepped the lowest line of the deck and the 
continuation of that line parallel to the upper part of the deck is taken as 
the freeboard deck. 


(ii) when a lower deck is designated as the freeboard deck, that part of the 
hull which extends above the freeboard deck is treated as a 
superstructure so far as concerns the application of the conditions of 
assignment and the calculation of freeboard. It is from this deck that the 
freeboard is calculated. 


(iii) when a lower deck is designated as the freeboard deck, such deck as a 
minimum shall consist of suitably framed stringers at the vessel sides 
and transversely at each watertight bulkhead which extends to the upper 
deck, within cargo spaces. 


The width of these stringers shall not be less than can be conveniently fitted 
having regard to the structure and the operation of the vessel. Any 
arrangement of stringers shall be such that structural requirement can also 
be met. 

(c) Discontinuous freeboard deck, stepped freeboard deck. 


(i) Where a recess in the freeboard deck extends to the sides of the vessel 
and is in excess of one metre in length, the lowest line of the exposed 


66 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 


deck and the continuation of that line parallel to the upper part of the deck 
is taken as the freeboard deck 


(ii) Where a recess in the freeboard deck does not extend to the sides of the 
vessel, the upper part of the deck is taken as the freeboard deck. 


(iii) Recesses not extending from side to side in a deck below the exposed 
deck, designated as the freeboard deck, may be disregarded, provided all 
openings in the weather deck are fitted with weather tight closing 
appliances. 


(iv) Due regard shall be given to the drainage of exposed recesses and to free 
surface effects on stability. 


(v) Provisions of sub-clause (i) through (iv) are not intended to apply to 
dredgers, hopper barges or other similar types of vessels with large open 
holds, where each case requires individual consideration. 


({0) “full superstructure” is a superstructure which, as a minimum, extends from the 
forward to the after perpendicular; 


() “forecastle” is a superstructure which extends from the forward perpendicular aft 
to a point which is forward of the after perpendicular, which may originate from a 
point forward of the forward perpendicular; 


({2) “height of superstructure" means the least vertical height measured at side from 
the top of the superstructure deck beams to the top of the freeboard deck beams; 


(3) "length" or the symbol (L) means ninety-six per cent... of the total length on a water 
line at eighty-five per cent of the least moulded depth measured from top of the keel, 
or the length from the force side of the stem to the axis of the rudder stock on the 
water line if that be greater. 


For vessels without a rudder stock, the length (L) is to be taken as 96% of the 
waterline at 85% of the least moulded depth.

| | 
torwars torrinal of 


the total length (FP) 


Where the stem contour is concave above the waterline at 85% of the least moulded 
depth, both the forward terminal of the total length and the fore-side of the stem 
respectively shall be taken at the vertical projection to that waterline of the aftermost 
point of the stem contour 


[भाग ॥--खण्ड 3(i)] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 67
